## 1. Metric and scoring quirks

| ID | Fact | Source (date) |
|---|---|---|
| F-MET-01 | score = adjusted edge Jaccard + 0.1 x division Jaccard; official code github.com/royerlab/kaggle-cell-tracking-competition | CONTEXT §1 l.10, §4 (06 Sep); `research/wf_ideas_v3/FINAL_REPORT.md` §b (25 Sep) |
| F-MET-02 | Node matching: optimal one-to-one bipartite assignment on scaled centroid distance, max 7.0 um | CONTEXT §4 l.43; `research/wf_ideas_v3/browser_intel.md` §5 (Overview, read 25 Sep 11:20 UTC) |
| F-MET-03 | Edge TP needs both endpoints matched to GT nodes linked in GT; edge FP only if an endpoint matches a GT node linked elsewhere -> edges/forks among UNANNOTATED cells are free **[c6]** Exact official rule (`tools/official/src/tracking_cellmot/metrics.py` l.157-195): a predicted edge is evaluable (can be FP) iff its SOURCE matches a GT node with out-degree > 0 OR its TARGET matches a GT node with in-degree > 0 (`pred_valid = out_valid \| in_valid`); a 'both ends matched' shortcut undercounts FP ~20x (6bba normal transitions 20 vs 1,135 FP); with the correct rule a CPU matcher reproduces the exp60 lab FN 1,705 / FP 1,681. The same function keeps at most 2 children per source (the two lowest edge ids) with a warning | **[c6]** `tools/official/src/tracking_cellmot/metrics.py` l.140-195 (read 26 Sep 06:2xZ); `research/wf_ideas_v4/round6/scan_recheck.json` (official edge FP rule finding); round6 `report.json` new_facts (8); CONTEXT §4 l.43-44|
| F-MET-04 | Node multiplier adj = J x (1 - 0.1 (N_pred - N_est)/N_est): linear, no kink, no upper clamp -> fewer nodes than N_est is a bonus; scores can exceed 1.0 **[c11]** Round 11 metric re-audit: the node-drop bonus is ~4.5e-6 per dropped node against an expected cost of ~9.5e-5, so dropping a node pays only at P(annotated) < ~0.2%; no unexploited metric lever **[c12]** The reach0955 retrain skeptic applies the law as '+1% nodes ~ -0.001' (so +3% nodes ~ -0.003; our exp23 secondary swap added +3.9% nodes and lost 0.007 on LB) and tightens any secondary-swap dummy node gate to [-2%, +1%] of the K2 dummy's 121,252 nodes. Forum players report that the LB moves with the predicted node count rather than with local edge J (C-89) **[c13]** Round-13 lab (exp60/exp64 CPU replays, in-sample for detector and edge net): a free 1% node drop is worth +0.0009 (44b6 +0.0006), a random 1% drop -0.019; the break-even enrichment r* ~0.03-0.05 is roughly density-free; the best in-lab pruning reaches +0.0015 lab (~+0.00025 public), below the R8 bar **[c26]** The 12:23 risk-stack line applies the law directly: adjusted edge J = J x (1 - 0.1 (T_pred - T_true)/T_true), so -0.73% nodes is a mechanical bonus of ~+0.0007. Round 26: K's READMIT adds +0.84% nodes, ~-0.0008 on any embryo (so the raw public K gain was ~+0.0028); DIS 2.25 on the K base is -0.68% nodes, ~+0.0006 (the dummy lab already contains this node term) | **[c12]** `research/wf_ideas_v4/reach0955/v_retrain.json` no_go_items, corrections (3); CONTEXT 20:51 l.2425-2436 (5); `research/wf_ideas_v4/reach0955/discussions_2026-09-26.notes.md`; **[c11]** `research/wf_ideas_v4/round11/report.json` new_facts (6); R11-A1 CONTEXT_TEXT (5); CONTEXT §4 l.45; `research/wf_ideas_v3/FINAL_REPORT.md` §j.1; browser_intel §5; `experiments/ext_biohub-exp003-two-embryos/NOTES.md` #4; **[c13]** CONTEXT 01:31 ROUND 13 RECORD (6) l.2658-2662; `research/wf_ideas_v4/round13/report.json` new_facts (8); `research/wf_ideas_v4/round13/scan_hyp_node_count.json` **[c26]** CONTEXT 12:23 l.3887-3889; `research/wf_ideas_v4/round26/report.json` new_facts (10) |

It must be logged in CONTEXT before any ladder score (R14-A2 (2), proposed; C-104). A ref COMPLETE before the watcher's first read with no S0 is AMBIGUOUS. **[c15]** APPLIED: S0 = the 0147Z snapshot was logged in CONTEXT 02:28 (2) and pushed 02:29:01Z, before any score.
